- **hekouwang-claude-md-doctor-skill**: 会勇禾口王的AI笔记 · Agent 运行时配置体检器。检查项目的 AGENTS.md（跨 Agent 推荐） 或 CLAUDE.md（及子目录本地配置）是否符合"把它当运行时配置、不是项目说明书"的最佳实践， 给出评分卡 + 按优先级的修复建议，并可代为修复。触发：用户说「检查我的 CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md / 运行时配置体检 / claude-md-doctor / agents.md 规范吗 / audit CLAUDE.md / lint AGENTS.md / 看看我的 agent 配置合不合规」。 任何"评估/审查/优化某个项目 AGENTS.md 或 CLAUDE.md 质量"的请求都应触发。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-claude-md-doctor-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-claude-md-doctor-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-claude-skill-doctor-skill**: 会勇禾口王的AI笔记 · Agent Skill（SKILL.md）体检器。检查一个 Claude/Agent Skill 是否 符合"按需加载的指令包，不是单文件巨石"的最佳实践——评 description 触发质量、SKILL.md 篇幅、渐进披露（references/ 拆分）、脚本外置、可移植性（无硬编码绝对路径）、安全（无硬编码 密钥、宿主元数据与多 Skill 发现冲突），给出评分卡 + 按优先级的修复建议，并可代为重构。触发：用户说「检查我的 skill / SKILL.md 体检 / 这个 skill 规范吗 / claude-skill-doctor / audit skill / lint SKILL.md / 我的 skill 太长了 / skill 拆分 / 看看我的 skill 合不合规 / skill 对齐官方规范」。 任何"评估/审查/优化某个 Agent Skill 质量或结构"的请求都应触发。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-claude-skill-doctor-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-claude-skill-doctor-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **hekouwang-env-doctor-skill**: 开发环境与磁盘体检：默认只读扫描（不读用户文档内容、不联网、不上报），按数据/缓存/残留三分法给出判定与官方清理建议。clean 子命令只跑各工具官方的缓存与旧版本清理命令（npm、yarn、pnpm、brew、pip、uv），逐项确认后执行；不删除版本管理器目录、不删模型权重与容器数据；被判为残留的版本管理器只在报告里给手动卸载步骤。附 scripts/run-all-doctors.sh：会调用 claude-md-doctor 与 skill-doctor，额外报告配置与技能分数，属附加能力。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-env-doctor-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-env-doctor-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-harness-check-skill**: 当用户要求检查、体检、回归、验收、解释或诊断会勇禾口王内容工作流 Harness，或维护、移植、打包、开源这个 Harness 检查器 Skill 时使用。统一调用 verify.sh 及治理、Hook、安全门、Episode 等检查，分别报告本地、暂存区、CI 与真实宿主边界。具体 EP 的文章、实验选型和渠道交付预检由仓内 hekouwang-precheck 负责。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-harness-check-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-harness-check-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-stock-data-reader-skill**: 会勇禾口王 · 个股公开数据速读 Skill。输入一个 A 股代码，自动用 akshare 拉取「资金流 / 龙虎榜 / 估值band / 财务三表 / 主营构成 / 北向 / 日K」等公开数据，算出固定指标体系 （价格轨迹·主力进出·席位结构·利润质量·利润结构·估值·风险线索），并产出「中立事后复盘」 形态的财经贴图 / 公众号长文 / 头条三件套（V2 米白或 V3 财经风）。内置金融合规护栏： 不荐股、不预测涨跌、不给买卖点，自动套风险提示条。 触发词：个股数据速读 / 扒公司公开数据 / 资金面复盘 / 出个股报告 / 3天一只 / stock-data-reader / 给我一只股的数据体检 / 用 akshare 拉某股数据。 ⚠️ 个股走公众号/头条为主；小红书仅限"中立复盘/算账/名词科普"形态且守合规护栏（不荐股/不预测/零站外导流），别发以资金流·席位为钩子的机构观感版。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-stock-data-reader-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-stock-data-reader-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **hekouwang-visual-dev-skill**: 维护会勇禾口王 Visual Core 的正式主题注册表、设计 Token、字体合同、完整画布背景、基础组件与 fit/shoot 运行时，并检查跨 Skill 所有权和注册表漂移。当用户要求调整共享视觉能力或核对 Visual Core、Content Master 与渠道 Skill 的契约时使用。五类实验台和当期选型归 Content Master，XHS 的 visual-plan.json 归 XHS Theme；本 Skill 不制作单期 EP、文章或渠道图片。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-visual-dev-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-visual-dev-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hekouwang-yandu-deck-skill**: 会勇禾口王的AI笔记 ·「演读 DECK」沉浸式演示产线与发布 Skill。把一篇文章/选题做成「一屏一镜、可翻页、能自动播放」的 keynote 演示版网页（默认米白引擎，国标系列走 V6 焰彩白；暖黑/焰彩黑为存量历史态），并自托管字体、发布到 Cloudflare Pages（hekouwang.pages.dev）。 当需要：① 把某篇文章/某期内容做成「演示版 / 演读 DECK / 翻页演示 / keynote 网页 / 沉浸式阅读页」；② 给「演读 DECK」站加一期演示、加一个系列、改首页；③ 给某个系列换主色/换肤（白↔黑 token-flip、切 V6 焰彩）；④ 发布/更新 hekouwang.pages.dev；⑤ 自托管字体、解决首屏字重跳变(FOUT)、子集化思源字体；⑥ 站点留言板（Cloudflare Pages Functions + D1）加装/排错 时使用。 触发词：演读DECK / 演读 deck / 演示版 / 翻页演示 / keynote 网页 / 沉浸式阅读 / 发布到 hekouwang / 加一期演示 / 演示版引擎 / deck换肤 / 焰彩deck / V6焰彩白 / token-flip / deck留言板。 ([huiyonghkw/hekouwang-yandu-deck-skill](https://github.com/huiyonghkw/hekouwang-yandu-deck-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **held-cards**: Hold new backlog cards while the host is busy, and file them all later in one lane and one PR (operator handoff rule 21). Use when you want to file a card but the host is busy or the PR queue is growing ("hold this card", "add to the cards-to-file list", "note a card for later"), to check whether it is quiet enough to file ("can we file the held cards?", "is the host quiet?"), and to file the held list ("file the held cards", "flush cards-to-file"). NOT for a blocking problem (main red, a stuck PR, a daemon down): that is filed and fixed now via `file-item`. ([chalbert/web-everything](https://github.com/chalbert/web-everything/tree/HEAD/skills-src/held-cards) · 2 copies)

- **held-orientation-and-visibility**: 薄片或带可见标记的载荷在取放期间保留本轮初始面向和公开要求的朝向，不为了自己读图方便而倾转。用已有侧视、放大公开RGB或空闲臂视角检查；观察臂先确认空手、无支撑职责且全臂通道安全。只有公开目标确实需要转向或必要净空要求、且方向变化被允许时才改变持物姿态。空手改善视野的倾斜方法不能直接套在已持载荷上。比较本体的面、主轴与目标，不能把法兰角度或抓点居中当作载荷朝向已正确。 ([manifoldai-research/R2SI](https://github.com/manifoldai-research/R2SI/tree/HEAD/XPolicyLab/policy/WorldScape_R2SI/harness/vendor/policy/RoboRSI_RoboProbe/skills/generic/held-orientation-and-visibility) · ★12)
- **held-out**: Use when reporting how well anything fitted or tuned performs — a model, forecast, classifier, heuristic, prompt, or threshold. The score counts only on data the thing never saw, against a bar set before scoring, a naive baseline, and a placebo. Use when a score came from the data used to build it, or when something was iterated until the eval passed. ([mikestangdevs/craft-skills](https://github.com/mikestangdevs/craft-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rigor/held-out) · ★4)
- **held-out-cell-type-ablation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a Symphony reference mapping flags query cell populations absent from the reference as novel, or when reproducing the ablation benchmark of per-cell/per-cluster mapping-confidence metrics (AUCs, Supplementary Fig. 9). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-cell-type-ablation-benchmarking))
- **held-out-cell-validation-model-selection**: Use when training a custom cTP-net model on paired scRNA-seq/CITE-seq data and you must pick optimal network weights via held-out cells rather than training loss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-cell-validation-model-selection))
- **held-out-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when a trained sequence-based classifier (e.g. BOM/XGBoost on motif counts) must be scored on a held-out test set of labeled CRE sequences, and per-class and aggregate performance metrics are needed for validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/held-out-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **held-out-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when a labeled categorical class label (e.g. UCI wine quality ratings) must be predicted on unseen samples and you need train/test-disjoint accuracy and F1 for a tuned pre-processing + feature-selection + estimator pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/held-out-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **held-out-condition-splits-evaluation**: Use when evaluating whether a multi-condition scRNA-seq model predicts held-out condition combinations (e.g. &lt;ctrl,severe&gt; cells) correctly, comparing against baselines like scGen and scPreGAN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-condition-splits-evaluation))
- **held-out-donor-validation-design**: Use when validating that a reference-mapping pipeline (e.g., Symphony) can transfer annotations (such as surface proteins) to query cells lacking the modality, and you need a donor-level split so query donors are never seen during reference construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/held-out-donor-validation-design))
- **held-out-gated-skill-patching**: Evolve an agent's skill/prompt package after repeated failures by tracing each failure to one component, patching only that component, and admitting the patch only if it beats the prior version on held-out instances.
